		"player_center_nav_faq" "FAQ"
		"player_center_faq_title" "Frequently Asked Questions"
		"player_center_faq_subtitle" "{count} entries. Select a question to view its answer."
		"player_center_faq_empty" "No FAQ entries are configured."
		"faq_q1" "What counts as a control effect in Blessing descriptions?"
		"faq_a1" "Control effects include stun, hex, root, fear, taunt, freeze, and being unable to act.<br><font color='#8e8e8e'>Adding a control effect, or reapplying control so that the existing effect is refreshed, counts as applying a control effect. Merely extending an existing control's duration without triggering a refresh does not count again.</font>"
		"faq_q2" "What counts as an ability hit in Blessing descriptions?"
		"faq_a2" "After an ability is cast (toggling an ability does not count as a cast), the first damage it deals to each enemy target counts as an ability hit. One cast can hit multiple targets, but can trigger only once per target. Casting the same ability again resets its hit record, so lingering damage may consume the newest cast's record."
		"faq_q3" "What are penalty counts?"
		"faq_a3" "Leaving early adds <font color='#83d18a'>5</font> penalty counts, with no current stack limit. Each of the first <font color='#83d18a'>5</font> daily victory reward settlements removes <font color='#83d18a'>1</font> penalty count and consumes that reward. Rewards are granted normally when no penalty counts remain."
		"faq_q4" "How do temporary Spell Amplification effects such as Magical Girl and Coin Toss work?"
		"faq_a4" "Temporary bonuses are recorded separately for each ability. Casting an ability locks in that cast's temporary bonus; until that same ability is cast again, all damage whose source is that ability receives the bonus. Casting another ability does not clear it. The next cast of the same ability replaces the recorded value.<br>For example, after Sand King casts Epicenter, later Epicenter pulses created by Aghanim's Shard keep that cast's temporary amplification until Epicenter is cast again. The Blessing description still determines its exact trigger and consumption rules."
		"faq_q5" "Do all players receive Blessings of the same quality?"
		"faq_a5" "Normally, the base quality for each level-up three-choice round is generated once at match start and is shared by all players. Personal Blessings or special effects can then increase a player's final quality, so the displayed quality is not always guaranteed to match. Gift boxes, random grants, and other non-level-up sources follow their own rules."
		"faq_q6" "What is the difference between Spell Amplification, Damage Output, Damage Taken, Base Attack Damage %, and Total Attack Damage %?"
		"faq_a6" "Spell Amplification increases ability damage affected by Spell Amplification; Damage Output modifies all damage you deal; Damage Taken modifies damage you receive. These are separate multiplicative categories.<br><font color='#83d18a'>Base Attack Damage %</font> is calculated from the hero's white base damage and appears as additional green damage, matching Dota 2's native concept.<br><font color='#83d18a'>Total Attack Damage %</font> is calculated from the sum of white base damage and existing green bonus damage, then adjusts that total proportionally."
		"faq_q7" "What is the difference between a \"basic attack\" and \"an attack landing\"?"
		"faq_a7" "A “basic attack” means the hero actively performs the complete basic attack flow. “An attack landing” means any attack record actually hits its target; this is broader and includes forced attacks triggered by abilities such as Swashbuckle and Stifling Dagger. Therefore, a Blessing that says “basic attack” normally will not trigger from those forced attacks, while one that says “when an attack lands” can, provided its target, cooldown, and other conditions are met."
		"faq_q8" "Are Refresh Tokens refunded if a match is not settled correctly?"
		"faq_a8" "For any match that is not settled correctly, including a room crash or every player on one team abandoning the match within a specific time, all Refresh Tokens spent are automatically refunded several hours after the match ends, once the server has verified the result."
